## Feature entry points

| Feature | Start reading at | What it covers |
|---|---|---|
| Extension entry | `index.js` | What SillyTavern loads for Story Director. |
| Module registration | `src/v2/module.js` | Registers Story Director's parts, screens and queries with Story Block. |
| Writes to story state | `src/v2/writes.js` | The commands that change what Story Director tracks. |
| Story arcs | `src/v2/arc/controller.js` | Browser-side arc planner and its draft, auto-accept and turn triggers. |
| Threads | `src/v2/threads.js` | Tracked story threads and their outcomes. |
| Map and places | `src/v2/atlas/map.js` | Builds the map widget props: places, routes, layers and discovery. |
| Cast | `src/v2/cast/apply.js` | Applies cast sync, advance, meet, withdraw and rumor steps. |
| Key people | `src/v2/keypeople/index.js` | Key-person tracking and its link to NPC State Delta. |
| Chronicle | `src/v2/chronicle/index.js` | The running record of story days and what happened. |
| Time and weather | `src/v2/time/index.js` | Story clock, days advancing, recaps and weather. |
| Checks and danger | `src/v2/checks/index.js` | Story checks, danger words and rolls. |

## One job per file: backlog

Files with more than 4 exports (legacy; pure re-export facades are exempt; split when moving out of `packages/`).
